# Diffusion Planner CAMP Integration V13 Audit

Date: 2026-06-27

This file is the current short-form audit entry point for the CAMP integration
with the fixed TiERIV Diffusion Planner. It replaces v12 for new current-state
writes because the user clarified that broader training data should come from
more fixed-DP candidate-set generation, not from CAMP-generated or modified
trajectories.

## Current Authority

## Data Definition And Authorization

The training datum remains a current route/state tick plus the fixed DP
candidate trajectory set for that tick. Expanding data means running the fixed
black-box DP candidate generator on more nonformal route/seed/scenario
combinations. CAMP still only ranks candidate indices by a linear score over
current-tick candidate atoms.

```text
user_authorized_fixed_dp_candidate_generation_now=True
user_authorized_camp_retraining_after_preflight=True

dataset_expansion_axis=more_fixed_dp_candidate_sets

camp_role=current_tick_fixed_candidate_reranker

score_expression=score_k(w)=a_k^T w
```

## V12 Probe Scale Finding

The v12 collection was still running when v13 was queued. A read-only scan of
the completed v12 fixed-DP selection logs showed that fallback-risk training
records are only the all-infeasible subset, so raw tick count is not equal to
training record count.

```text

v13_expected_fallback_records_approx=1746
fallback_risk_training_records_scope=records_without_feasible_candidate_only
```

## Larger Fixed DP Candidate Collection Launch

The v13 collection was launched as a queued serial AutoDL job. It waits for the
v12 collection summary before starting so fixed-DP replay does not run
concurrently with the existing v12 replay loop. The v13 pipeline was launched
at the same time and waits for the v13 collection summary before building the
dataset, validating contracts, splitting, fitting train-only scales, running
the sufficiency preflight, and training the offline nonpromotion static CAMP
artifact.


## Candidate Collection Contract

Every generated replay must remain inside the fixed-DP candidate contract. The
collection is useful only if the resulting `collection_summary.json` confirms
these values.

```text

must_validate_formal_seed_path_matches=0
```

## Completed V12 And V13 Evidence

The v12 intermediate collection and the v13 large collection both completed on
AutoDL after the v13 launch. The AutoDL CAMP worktree was intentionally kept at
the v13 launch runner commit until all replay and training jobs stopped. After
both v13 replay and pipeline were complete, AutoDL CAMP was fast-forwarded to
the GitHub/local HEAD using a verified local git bundle because direct GitHub
HTTPS fetch from AutoDL timed out. This did not modify Diffusion Planner.

## V13 Large Collection Result

The v13 collection produced substantially more fixed-DP candidate-set training
coverage than the earlier tiny fallback-risk artifact. All records kept the
fixed candidate tensor contract: K=8, no CAMP candidate generation, no trajectory
mutation, no guidance/reference blend, no DP modification, and no formal seeds.

## V13 Fallback-Risk Training Result

The v13 pipeline built the fallback-risk dataset from fixed DP selection logs,
validated the contract, split by record identity, fitted scales on training
groups only, passed preflight, and trained an offline nonpromotion static CAMP
reranker. These artifacts are not deployment artifacts and do not authorize
selector or atom promotion.

## Current Result Boundary

The v13 artifact is a larger offline nonpromotion static CAMP reranker trained
from fixed DP candidate sets. It remains a development artifact. No online
selector has been changed, no atom has been promoted, no checkpoint has been
declared deployable, and no safety or CAMP-over-DP Top-1 claim is authorized.

## V13 Default-Off Shadow Selector Implementation

The default-off shadow selector implementation gate was executed after explicit
user authorization for
`dp_camp_v13_default_off_shadow_selector_implementation_only_after_explicit_user_authorization`.
It added a production runner path for a v13 shadow selector that is disabled by
default and fail-closed. When enabled, the runner first checks the immutable
artifact hash contract for the atom scales and static weights/checkpoint. If
the contract is missing or mismatched, candidate count is not K=8, selector
mode is not static, or selector artifact loading fails, the runner does not
install the CAMP predictor and records DP Top-1 fail-closed shadow status in
the summary. If the contract is ready, the runner computes CAMP scores over the
fixed current-tick DP candidate tensor, records `shadow_selected_index`, but
forces `selected_index` and `executed_index` to DP candidate 0. This implements
shadow observability only; it does not route CAMP selection into execution.

This gate did not run replay, generate candidates, train CAMP, modify DP,
promote atoms or selectors, deploy, change the online selector, or authorize
safety/CAMP-over-DP claims.

## V13 Default-Off Shadow Selector Post-Implementation Static Contract Review

The post-implementation static contract review was executed after the actual
default-off shadow selector implementation and focused unit tests were merged
and synchronized to AutoDL. The review inspected the production replay runner,
the focused shadow-selector unit tests, the Benders atom contract tests, and
this v13 audit. It confirmed that the implementation remains default-off,
fail-closed, records only `shadow_selected_index`, forces executed selection to
DP candidate 0 during the shadow phase, preserves fixed-candidate affine
scoring `score_k(w)=a_k^T w`, rejects execution-changing flag combinations,
and keeps promotion/deployment/replay/candidate-generation/DP-modification
boundaries closed.

materialize an artifact manifest, enable the runtime shadow selector, promote

## V13 Default-Off Shadow Selector Runtime Artifact Manifest Materializer Implementation

The runtime artifact manifest materializer implementation-only gate added a
default-off, fail-closed materializer script and focused unit tests. The
materializer is designed to read one materialization plan only when explicitly
enabled, verify the expected plan SHA256, verify the fixed DP head, verify the
planned output path, verify `atom_scales` and `static_weights` files and
hashes, and then write exactly one runtime artifact manifest JSON matching the
runner lookup contract. The unit tests exercise this behavior on synthetic
temporary artifacts only.

This implementation gate did not materialize the real runtime manifest, execute
replay, enable the shadow selector, generate candidates, train CAMP, modify DP,
promote atoms or selectors, deploy, or authorize safety/CAMP-over-DP claims.

The post-implementation static contract review for the runtime artifact
manifest materializer completed on AutoDL at CAMP commit
`8b16bbf0e6748090e1c05f74c199e21488fb8964` with fixed DP commit
`7a1d33da277a1992ec474b5383a0c963c72e04e4`. The review inspected the
materialization plan, materializer source, focused tests, replay runner manifest
lookup contract, and this audit record. It verified that the materializer is

## V13 Default-Off Shadow Selector Runtime Artifact Manifest Materialization

The runtime artifact manifest materialization gate executed the reviewed
default-off materializer on AutoDL at CAMP commit
`e10a8cb7afed8900683c7629f2becaf4003a2963` with fixed DP commit
`7a1d33da277a1992ec474b5383a0c963c72e04e4`. The materializer read the approved
materialization plan, verified the plan SHA256, verified the fixed DP head,
verified that the planned output did not already exist, verified the
`atom_scales` and `static_weights` artifact hashes, and wrote exactly one
runtime artifact manifest JSON at the planned path.

The materialized manifest is still default-off and non-promotional. It preserves
the fixed DP candidate reranking boundary: CAMP only scores existing DP
candidates with the affine atom vector, the executed output policy remains
`dp_top1`, and no trajectory generation, modification, blending, guidance,
postprocessing, replay, training, DP modification, promotion, deployment, or
safety/CAMP-over-DP claim was executed or authorized by this gate.

## V13 Default-Off Shadow Selector Runtime Shadow Replay Preflight

The shadow replay preflight inspected the materialized runtime manifest, runner
CLI/source contract, v13 collection command provenance, and a single previously
successful non-formal replay configuration. It prepared one default-off shadow
replay smoke command for `sample_normal`, seed 301, `npc_0`, `tl_off`. Seed 301
is not one of the frozen formal seeds 11/12/13. The command uses fixed DP black
box candidate generation with K=8 and runs CAMP only as a default-off shadow
reranker over the current-tick finite candidate set; the executed trajectory
must remain DP Top-1 and the shadow selector may only log the shadow selected
index and diagnostics.

This preflight did not execute replay, generate candidates, train CAMP, modify
DP, promote atoms or selectors, deploy, or authorize safety/CAMP-over-DP
claims. It authorizes only the planned single non-formal default-off shadow
replay smoke execution as the next gate.

## V13 Default-Off Shadow Selector Runtime Shadow Replay Smoke Execution

The single non-formal default-off shadow replay smoke command from the
preflight executed successfully on AutoDL at CAMP commit
`84b696d0ad56458d1d09871319ffc169e4f53a2f` with fixed DP commit
`7a1d33da277a1992ec474b5383a0c963c72e04e4`. The run used
`sample_normal`, seed 301, `npc_0`, `tl_off`, 100 replay steps, K=8, the
materialized runtime manifest, and the static CAMP weights/scales. Formal seeds
11/12/13 were not used.

The smoke run produced 100 selection records and 100 default-off shadow selector
records. All shadow records had artifact contract ready, no fail-closed reason,
`selection_effect=False`, `executed_output_policy=dp_top1`, affine
`score_k(w)=a_k^T w`, and fixed DP candidate reranking only. The executed index
and recorded selected index were always 0, while the shadow selected index was
logged separately and was nonzero for 96 records. This is plumbing evidence only:
it does not authorize safety/CAMP-over-DP claims, selector promotion, deployment,
DP modification, or CAMP training.

## V13 Default-Off Shadow Selector Broader Nonformal Shadow Replay Batch Preflight

The broader nonformal shadow replay batch preflight prepared 32 default-off
shadow replay commands on AutoDL at CAMP commit
`72baf9855178adc04665dbfbf3e3c3c57b9b2a22` with fixed DP commit
`7a1d33da277a1992ec474b5383a0c963c72e04e4`. The batch covers four routes
(`sample_normal`, `sample_tl`, `nishi_release`, `nishi_lane_change`), two
non-formal seeds (301, 302), two NPC settings (0, 4), and both traffic-light
modes (`on`, `off`) for 100 replay steps per command.

The preflight verified that formal seeds 11/12/13 are excluded, all planned
output paths are absent, the materialized runtime manifest hash matches, the
static atom scales and weights match the manifest, each command uses
`--camp_default_off_shadow_selector` with `--camp_shadow_artifact_manifest`, no
guidance/reference-blend/postselection/relaxation/splice flags are present, and
the runner still forces default-off shadow execution to DP Top-1 while recording
only the shadow selected index. This gate did not execute the batch, train CAMP,
modify DP, promote, deploy, or authorize safety/CAMP-over-DP claims.

## V13 Default-Off Shadow Selector Broader Nonformal Shadow Replay Batch Execution

The broader nonformal default-off shadow replay batch executed all 32 preflight
commands on AutoDL at CAMP commit
`aa04ff6cc818d3f2fc44a285a1848ca89d62a355` with fixed DP commit
`7a1d33da277a1992ec474b5383a0c963c72e04e4`. The batch covered four routes
(`sample_normal`, `sample_tl`, `nishi_release`, `nishi_lane_change`), two
non-formal seeds (301, 302), two NPC settings (0, 4), and both traffic-light
modes (`on`, `off`) for 100 replay steps per command.

The validation passed with 32/32 completed commands, 3200 selection records,
3200 shadow records, zero command failures, zero failed shadow records, executed
index always 0, recorded selected index always 0, and shadow scores never routed
to execution. The shadow reranker selected nonzero candidate indices 2505 times,
which confirms the shadow selection path is live while the executed trajectory
remains DP Top-1. This gate did not train CAMP, modify DP, promote a selector,
deploy, or authorize safety/CAMP-over-DP claims.

## V13 Broader Nonformal Shadow Replay Batch Result Review

The broader nonformal shadow replay batch result review aggregated the completed
32-command default-off shadow replay evidence on AutoDL at CAMP commit
`df09abf213e8500a4e7e42e68ad9e345ec795ada` with fixed DP commit
`7a1d33da277a1992ec474b5383a0c963c72e04e4`. The review confirmed that the
shadow selector path is live (2505/3200 records selected a nonzero candidate in
shadow) while every executed trajectory and recorded selected index remained DP
Top-1.

This review interprets the batch as runtime shadow evidence only, not as a new
training dataset, promotion basis, deployable checkpoint, or safety/CAMP-over-DP
claim. It confirms the standing user authorization for future CAMP training and
routes the next work to a concrete preflight for larger non-formal fixed-DP
candidate-set expansion and retraining, without executing that expansion or
training in this gate.

## V13 Nonformal Fixed-DP Candidate Expansion Retraining Preflight

The candidate expansion and retraining preflight prepared an execution plan on
AutoDL at CAMP commit `d0706908e9d13d28040854776c62fd1885a9c68a` with fixed
DP commit `7a1d33da277a1992ec474b5383a0c963c72e04e4`. It reuses the completed
v13 base collection and training pipeline, then plans an addon collection over
non-formal seeds 333-364 for the same four routes, two NPC settings, and two
traffic-light modes. The addon is 512 fixed-DP candidate replay commands and
51200 ticks; combined with the base v13 collection it predeclares 1024 selection
logs and 102400 ticks for fallback-risk retraining.

The preflight verified that base v13 artifacts are complete, formal seeds
11/12/13 are excluded, planned addon outputs are absent and unique, required
collection/training scripts and assets exist, DP remains fixed, CAMP remains a
fixed-candidate reranker only, and the Benders score contract remains
`score_k(w)=a_k^T w`. Because the user has authorized all subsequent training
tasks, the next gate may execute the fixed-DP addon candidate collection and
combined CAMP retraining without another training authorization prompt. It still
does not authorize CAMP candidate generation, trajectory modification, DP
modification, promotion, deployment, or safety/CAMP-over-DP claims.

## Current V13 Candidate Expansion And Retraining Execution Boundary

The current authorized next scope is execution-only for the predeclared
non-formal fixed-DP candidate expansion and combined CAMP retraining plan. That
gate may run the 512 addon fixed-DP replay commands, validate the addon
collection contract, combine the addon selection logs with the completed v13
base selection logs, build/validate the fallback-risk dataset, and execute CAMP
static fallback-risk retraining with the predeclared simplex/CVaR/L2 settings.
It must use only non-formal seeds, keep DP fixed, keep CAMP as a fixed-candidate
reranker, and preserve `score_k(w)=a_k^T w`. It must not promote atoms or
selectors, deploy, change the online selector, modify DP, generate or modify
trajectories with CAMP, or claim safety/CAMP-over-DP benefit.

## V13 Nonformal Fixed-DP Candidate Expansion Retraining Execution

The predeclared non-formal fixed-DP candidate expansion and combined CAMP
retraining execution completed on AutoDL. The execution ran at CAMP commit
`24d4a5465cf11f0014de92465d3211d4bfc872cc` with DP fixed at
`7a1d33da277a1992ec474b5383a0c963c72e04e4`. It executed the 512 addon
fixed-DP replay commands, combined the addon selection logs with the completed
v13 base collection, built and validated the fallback-risk dataset, and trained
the static CAMP reranker with the predeclared simplex/CVaR/L2 objective.

This is a real CAMP retraining execution, but it is still an offline,
non-promotion artifact. CAMP did not generate, rewrite, blend, guide,
postprocess, or postselect trajectories. The trained weights only score the
fixed DP candidate set with `score_k(w)=a_k^T w`; DP remains the fixed black-box
candidate trajectory generator. The execution does not authorize promotion,
deployment, online selector changes, formal-seed use, DP modification, or any
safety/CAMP-over-DP Top-1 claim.

## V13 Candidate Expansion Retraining Post-Training Audits

The post-training nonpromotion artifact audit and development holdout
acceptance audit were executed on AutoDL after local, GitHub, and AutoDL CAMP
were synchronized at `4c522f61f4a2101bf81a3fda002f0d624ad70db7`. These audits
read only the newly trained fixed-DP candidate reranker artifacts from the
candidate-expansion combined retraining pipeline. They did not run replay,
generate candidates, train CAMP again, modify DP, promote atoms or selectors,
deploy, or make safety/CAMP-over-DP Top-1 claims.

The nonpromotion audit verified the trained artifact hashes, simplex
nonnegative weights, JSON/NPY weight equality, strictly positive atom scales,
the approved 14-atom schema, and `score_k(w)=a_k^T w`. The development holdout
audit recomputed fixed-candidate validation-holdout scores over 5632 validation
records without feasible DP candidates; those metrics are diagnostic only and
are not safety or superiority claims.

## V13 Candidate Expansion Promotion Evidence Package Preflight

The candidate-expansion promotion evidence package preflight was executed on
AutoDL after local, GitHub, and AutoDL CAMP were synchronized at
`ce1cd14e59a03aa278a5c13a7d722a923dfef7a8`. It consumed only existing
candidate-expansion plan, result-review, collection, pipeline, training,
weights, scale, nonpromotion-audit, and holdout-audit artifacts. It did not
promote atoms or selectors, deploy a checkpoint, train CAMP, run replay,
generate candidates, modify DP, change online selector behavior, or authorize
safety/CAMP-over-DP claims.

artifact with 512 selection logs and 51,200 records; the combined pipeline and

preflight validates those roles separately so the addon collection source is

only gate was executed locally and on AutoDL after local, GitHub, and AutoDL

## V13 Candidate Expansion Default-Off Shadow Selector Implementation

The candidate-expansion default-off shadow selector implementation gate was
validated after explicit user authorization for
`dp_camp_v13_default_off_shadow_selector_implementation_only_after_explicit_user_authorization`.
The production implementation already existed in the current runner and no
additional production diff was required in this gate. The validated
implementation is default-off and fail-closed: when enabled, the runner checks
the immutable artifact hash contract for atom scales and static
weights/checkpoint, computes CAMP scores over the fixed current-tick DP
candidate tensor, records `shadow_selected_index`, and forces `selected_index`
and `executed_index` to DP candidate 0. This implements shadow observability
only; it does not route CAMP selection into execution.

This gate did not run replay, generate candidates, train CAMP, modify DP,
promote atoms or selectors, deploy, change the online selector default, or
authorize safety/CAMP-over-DP claims.
